> Closure Notes (2026-05-14, 2026-05-16 continued, 2026-05-17 re-audit): This table is a cumulative issue list automatically extracted from multiple rounds of review in `architecture-design-review.md`, with many cross-round duplicates. This round uses the final rounds A-G and the specific issues 741-783 at the end of the table as the main line for code, configuration, UI, documentation, and test closure; historical duplicates are unified into the same fix evidence. After the 2026-05-17 re-audit, the two "large test governance items" (703/726) originally in the table are no longer retained as "unclosed defects"; they are reclassified as `Re-audited (Governance Observation Items)` per the original nature in section 2.3 of `architecture-design-review.md`: the current precise count and the reason for not changing are preserved, but the repository-scale observations are not disguised as one-off code defects.

> Second Re-audit Boundary (2026-05-14, supplemented 2026-05-17): This round only resolves the documentation issues listed in the table and the minimal in-repo defects pointed to within the table, and does not add scope outside the table; large structural entries (giant file splits, script/directory count governance, global mock/any/TODO count governance, long-term cross-language documentation synchronization, etc.) have been handled as "governance items" and are no longer faked as "fixed" with templated evidence to pretend that the full code rework is complete. On 2026-05-17, the current repository's full test suite was re-run and the results were incorporated into this table's evidence.

> Third Re-audit Rebuild (2026-05-14): The 783 rows of original templated `Review Conclusion`, `Root Cause`, and `Evidence` columns were first cleared, then reclassified and refilled based on each row's area, issue, file path, and suggested fix; after the re-audit, the same reason is no longer used to batch-cover all issues.

> Fourth Authoritative Re-audit (2026-05-16): `node scripts/ci/audit-review-batch-resource-contracts.mjs`, `node scripts/ci/audit-codebase-inventory.mjs`, `node scripts/ci/audit-review-governance-closures.mjs`, and `npm audit --prefix ui` were re-executed; the current authoritative results are `118/118`, `11/11`, `9/9`, and `found 0 vulnerabilities`; root-level `:memory:*`, `artifacts/`, and `session-replay/` generated artifacts have been cleaned up. Some historical rows in the table retain intermediate counts from the batch re-audits at the time (such as `46/46`, `69/69`, `88/88`, `92/92`, `96/96`, `103/103`); these counts are no longer treated as the current authoritative conclusion and are unified with the above 2026-05-16 re-audit results as the standard.
